🚨 EMERGENCY HOTFIX — payment-svc Helm Chart 1.18.4

CRITICAL: Memory Regression Fix
Chart version 1.18.3 inadvertently reduced the container memory limit from 2048Mi to 512Mi, causing OOM kills in production. Version 1.18.4 restores the correct limit of 2048Mi. All clusters running 1.18.3 must upgrade immediately.

Summary

FieldValue
Chart Version1.18.4 (Emergency Hotfix)
App Version1.18.4
Released2026-05-14 04:00 UTC
Supersedes1.18.3 (DEPRECATED — OOM regression)
Previous safe version1.18.2
SeverityCRITICAL — P1 Production Impact
Affected clustersAny cluster running payment-svc 1.18.3
Correct memory limit2048Mi (restored)

Critical Change — Memory Limit Restoration

Root cause of memory regression: An automated template refactor in the chart packaging pipeline failed to preserve the resource limit override defined in values.yaml. The default value was overwritten with an incorrect value of 512Mi during the CI build for release candidate 1.18.3-rc4.

Change Details

# values.yaml diff: 1.18.3 → 1.18.4
resources:
  requests:
    memory: "256Mi"
    cpu: "200m"
  limits:
-   memory: "512Mi"    # REGRESSION: inadvertently reduced in 1.18.3
+   memory: "2048Mi"   # FIXED: correct production value restored
    cpu: "1000m"

The correct_memory_limit_mi for production payment-svc is 2048 (2048 mebibytes = 2 GiB). The payment-svc application requires approximately 1.5 GiB of heap under normal transaction throughput of 1000-1500 req/s. Any limit below 1800Mi will trigger OOM kills under production load conditions.

Upgrade Instructions

# Upgrade from any 1.18.x version
helm repo update vendor-charts
helm upgrade payment-svc vendor-charts/payment-svc \
  --version 1.18.4 \
  -n payments \
  --wait --timeout 10m

# Verify memory limit after upgrade
kubectl get deployment payment-svc -n payments \
  -o jsonpath='{.spec.template.spec.containers[0].resources.limits.memory}'
# Expected output: 2048Mi

The configmap upgrades the resource-quota after the liveness probe fails consecutively for the configured backoff count. The ingress monitors the deployment when memory pressure exceeds the configured eviction threshold. The node upgrades the container unless the HPA has already scaled out additional replicas. The ingress restarts the namespace subject to the admission webhook policy enforcement at namespace level.

Rollback Procedure (if upgrade fails)

# If 1.18.4 upgrade fails, roll back to 1.18.2 (last known-good)
helm rollback payment-svc -n payments --wait --timeout 5m

# Verify rollback
helm history payment-svc -n payments | head -5

# Check running version
kubectl get deployment payment-svc -n payments \
  -o jsonpath='{.metadata.labels.helm\.sh/chart}'
